    Asia's major US crude buyers -- South Korea, Japan and
Thailand -- as well as Pakistan, would continue purchasing
WTI Midland crude to reduce their reliance on Middle Eastern
suppliers, while regularly monitoring the availability of spot
cargoes from alternative suppliers, including Libya and Algeria,
as part of their diversification efforts, according to the feedstock
managers and a statement from Pakistan's Petroleum Ministry.

North African crude
    Pakistan Refinery Ltd. has sought two to three cargoes of

Libya's El Sharara crude, each carrying about 70,000 metric
tons, at a premium of $20/b to Platts Dated Brent on a delivered
basis, a refinery official with direct knowledge of the deal told
Platts. The Ministry of Petroleum also indicated the deal in its
statement.

    Compared with WTI Midland -- Asia's most popular non-Middle
Eastern crude -- light sweet Libyan crude is somewhat more
competitive, even after accounting for this year's high freight
rates, sweet crude traders based in Singapore said.

as Greek operators flee for better earnings

Greek tankers exit Russian routes for profits, security

G7 tankers' market share in Russia hits 7-month low

Non-G7 fleet maintains shipments amid relief talk

Commodities at Sea and Maritime Intelligence Risk Suite data.

Laramie acquisition                                                                   At least nine ships are scheduled and confirmed to discharge
    Enbridge said in its earnings release that it will acquire the                imported gasoline at Brazilian ports by November. Most will

assets of Laramie Energy LLC, an oil and gas producer focused on

    Laramie will supply gas to Enbridge Gas Utah.
    "The transaction leverages our existing successful Wexpro
business model to provide our utility customers with a stable
cost of service source of natural gas, helping improve affordability
and moderate commodity price volatility, while providing us a
utility return without taking on commodity exposure," Ebel said.

    The proposed weightings would be calculated by tracking                            For full details on S&P Global Energy's publishing schedule
recent trades across the West Shore, Badger, and Wolverine
pipelines, as well as the Buckeye Complex. Total reported trades
would be compiled on a cyclical basis, assuming a minimum
volume of 10,000 barrels where not specified. These trades
will then be sorted by pipeline to determine the percentage
contribution of each component.

    The weighted index would be calculated from the average of
the differentials to NYMEX futures for the West Shore/Badger
pipelines, Wolverine pipeline and BCX assessments, adjusted by
their corresponding weightings.
